# Review — 017-derive-dont-ask

## Summary

Five passes over 017's window since its reopen (diff-base d6d0affd, base-source reopen) to HEAD. The window holds 017's own commits, from its rule and scenario work through task 46's hook change (3cf8dffd), and the commits that touched 017's directory while naming other specs. Of the commits naming no spec, only b1d319f1's primitives.rs hunks were uncredited, since earlier committed reviews read the rest. Five agents each read the nine loaded rule files whole.

Coverage: examined counts 32 of the 34 scope entries, each read whole. unread names two:

- runtime/CHANGELOG.md, 436 KB: 0481c218's and 4fc6a591's hunks read;
- runtime/src/schema/primitives.rs, 459 KB: b1d319f1's hunks and the items they touch read.

Fixed in the run, seven chores:

- the staging test's unused tree_has helper is removed;
- the hook's comment on generator sources names scripts/maintainer-only-commands.txt;
- the a-commit-stages-only-its-own-generated-files scenario tells the old hook's behavior in the past tense, until 8de6b8a6;
- AC28 is ticked, its every clause holding in the tree;
- emit_result is emit_result_gated with a block that never fires;
- the apply-manifest and enforce-manifest hydrators use hydrate_field;
- primitives.rs' round-trip tests drop the Pi examples that name nothing a host writes. MergePermissionsArgs' doc comment keeps its rationale, which still holds.

Seven new observations are waived by the rule with their reasons. Of the stored decisions:

- 30 that the reviewers found still standing carry their stored outcomes, 29 waived and one deferred to 020's every-loaded-rule-file-has-a-pass scenario;
- 7 whose issue no longer stands are pruned;
- 63 on files outside the window are retained.

Low confidence: CFG-ENV-002 on docs/runtime.md's inventory, which omits the maintainer hook's DUCTUS_ALLOW_EMPTY and DUCTUS_SKIP_RUNTIME_CHECKS. Whether a maintainer-only hook is the application the rule means is the judgment.

process-waivers applied nothing: 017 stores no waivers.

1 fix round(s): 1 re-read, 0 diff-checked, 0 exempt, 0 not re-read.

## MUST violations (blocking)

*None.*

## SHOULD violations (advisory)

*None.*

## Low-confidence findings

### LOW-CONFIDENCE: CFG-ENV-002 — docs/runtime.md calls itself the one inventory of ductus's environment variables, yet this repo's pre-commit hook reads DUCTUS_ALLOW_EMPTY and DUCTUS_SKIP_RUNTIME_CHECKS, which it does not list

- **File**: `docs/runtime.md:35-35`
- **Rule**: Every environment variable and every configuration-file key the application reads MUST be listed in a canonical inventory maintained alongside the source code — one inventory for each kind of source, or one for both, and never two for the same source. The inventory MUST describe each value's purpose, declare whether it is required or optional, and provide a safe placeholder or default value. Acceptable forms include `.env.example` (projects that load configuration with a dotenv-style loader), a Helm chart's `values.yaml` (defaults) with its `values.schema.json` (types and required keys), a commented example of the configuration file that lists every key, a typed config module that lists every value, or an equivalent declarative manifest checked into the repository. Command-line flags are outside this rule: a command-line interface documents its flags in its own help output.
- **Finding**: The inventory's lead sentence scopes itself to the runtime, the installer and /ductus, and the maintainer script that regenerates the command copies, but it also lists DUCTUS_BIN, which .githooks/pre-commit sets for that script. The same hook reads DUCTUS_ALLOW_EMPTY (the empty-commit escape hatch; documented only in the hook's own comment and message) and DUCTUS_SKIP_RUNTIME_CHECKS (named in docs/developing-ductus.md only), and neither is in the table. Low confidence: whether a maintainer-only git hook is 'the application' under CFG-ENV-002 is a judgement, the variables predate the window, and the rule's Verification is written for specs and plans.
- **Auto-fixable**: no
- **Suggested fix**: Add two rows to docs/runtime.md's table (DUCTUS_SKIP_RUNTIME_CHECKS and DUCTUS_ALLOW_EMPTY, each 'Not read by the runtime', default unset, read by this repository's pre-commit hook), or narrow the lead sentence to exclude the repository's own hook.

## Waived findings

*None.*

## Deferred findings

*None.*

## Observations

- security: BE-API-015's handshake-URL MUST NOT also bars a CSRF token there, which the OWASP WebSocket Security Cheat Sheet suggests sending in the handshake — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: the owner's 2026-10-20 answer keeps every token out of the handshake URL, following the same cheat sheet's advice against tokens in URL query strings; (a)'s Origin allowlist is the handshake's cross-site defense, and a CSRF token can still be checked in the first message
- security: BE-AUTHN-018's newer-token invalidation would cancel a code issued by one method when a code by another is issued in the same recovery — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: a recovery that verifies the first code before issuing the second complies, since the first is already used; NIST SP 800-63B-4 sets no invalidation across codes and nothing in BE-AUTHN-018 forbids that order
- convention — `tree_has` is defined in the staging test and never called (dead helper; AGENTS.md: no dead code) — `scripts/tests/test-pre-commit-staging.sh:73` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — AC28 is unticked although every clause holds in the tree: CFG-ENV-013 (MUST) has Statement, Verification and Source in configuration-cross.md; CFG-ENV-009, CFG-ENV-010, BE-DATA-003, BE-DATA-008 and BE-DATA-012 each leave the case to it; data-model.md Rule set enumerates it with its tier. No task in tasks.md carries the tick — `specs/017-derive-dont-ask/spec.md:167` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — a-commit-stages-only-its-own-generated-files states the defect in the present tense, though the hook no longer behaves so: Context says the hook `runs ... over the working tree, then git adds every output path`, and the markdown Edge Case says `Today it runs markdownlint-cli2 --fix on the working copy and git adds the whole file` — `specs/017-derive-dont-ask/scenarios/a-commit-stages-only-its-own-generated-files.md:9` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- convention — the hook's comment says everything the three generators read is under framework/ or is one of their own scripts; gen-help-tables.sh also reads scripts/maintainer-only-commands.txt. It is read only for the coverage assertion (excluded_commands feeds expected_commands and uncovered, never a table row), so a staged change to it moves no generated output and a bad one fails the scratch run loudly; the claim is inexact, the behavior is sound — `.githooks/pre-commit:145` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — the hook's markdown stage skips silently when only the npx fallback is available or the local linter exits other than 1, so a lint that did not run looks like one that passed (constitution §design-principles); CI step (c) is the gate that catches it — `.githooks/pre-commit:227` — **waived**: the owner's decision of 2026-11-13 (017 task 46) keeps the npx fallback and a non-1 exit swallowed so a failed fetch never blocks a commit, the header comment says so, and framework-checks step (c) runs the pinned linter on every push; an observation about the pipeline's own machinery (decided by the rule)
- other — staged_names, staged_md and the changed-path loop parse `git diff --name-only` output, which core.quotepath quotes for non-ASCII or control characters, while the raw -z list is unquoted; a staged path so quoted would never match, so its scratch-run change would be dropped rather than carried — `.githooks/pre-commit:139` — **waived**: latent only: measured 0 of 1304 tracked paths contain a non-ASCII character, and a fix (`--name-only -z` throughout) is a design change to a hook the owner reshaped on 2026-11-11; an observation about the pipeline's own machinery (decided by the rule)
- other — the test holds no case for the hook's stated swallow branches (the npx fallback exiting 1, a local linter exit other than 1); the fixture installs a local stub, so removing the `mdlint_local` condition would break no test — `scripts/tests/test-pre-commit-staging.sh:318` — **waived**: QUAL-TEST-001 judges a test that exists against the behavior it names, and no test names this behavior; a new case is new work the owner has not asked for, and the branch's blast radius is a lint CI runs again; an observation about the pipeline's own machinery (decided by the rule)
- other — runtime/CHANGELOG.md's [Unreleased] prose says `/review` "applies Statements and no routing until spec 020's lands" (introduced by 0481c218, ~lines 962-969; the same future-tense claim also stands at ~787 and ~806, outside the window). Spec 020 task 15 (efe81579, 2026-11-11) landed the routing in framework/reference/review.md §Security pass ("Report a defect under its owner"), so the sentence is false at HEAD. It was true when 0481c218 wrote it; 020's commit did not sweep the changelog. — `runtime/CHANGELOG.md:962` — **waived**: The claim became stale by 020's later commit, not by 017's hunk; the entry is the Unreleased section whose own preamble says the release commit rewrites it, and a correct rewrite must first decide which pairs the landed routing now moves (configuration-cross.md is applied by no review pass), a judgement for 020's owner rather than a mechanical edit. (decided by the rule)
- other — primitives.rs: b1d319f1 replaced dropped-host examples with `.pi/settings.json` (merge-permissions test) and `.pi/acme-session.json` (migrate-session-file test) under comments still saying "A non-Claude host supplies its own settings path" and "Adopter on a non-Claude host"; Pi has no permission-gating settings file (framework/bootstrap/configure/pi.md) and the pre-0.10.0 legacy session JSON predates Pi support (064, 2026-10-05), so neither path names anything a host writes. MergePermissionsArgs' doc comment likewise keeps "silently writing to a Claude-shaped location on a non-Claude host". The assertions test only serialization, so nothing breaks. — `runtime/src/schema/primitives.rs:9101` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — emit_result repeats emit_result_gated's serialize/write/exit body; it is exactly emit_result_gated with a blocks closure that is always false. — `runtime/src/main.rs:417-436` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — hydrate_apply_manifest and hydrate_enforce_manifest predate hydrate_field and repeat its `if let Some(path) = ...clone() { field = load_cli_json(flag, &path)? }` shape five times, while the write-review, write-analysis, process-waivers, process-decisions and prune-project hydrators use the helper. — `runtime/src/main.rs:291-314` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — every_short_help_prints_on_one_line (the window's test) checks a subcommand's `about` and an argument's short help, but not `long_about` or an argument's long help, so a brace-wrapped n in a descriptions.rs constant (which is both `about`'s source and `long_about`) or in the later paragraph of a clap::Args doc comment would still split `--help` with every test green; descriptions.rs's module doc states the ban and nothing enforces it for the long form. No current violation: `{n}` occurs in runtime/src only in that module doc, this test, and three non-clap result-struct doc comments in schema/primitives.rs. — `runtime/src/main.rs:1400-1425` — **waived**: An enforcement gap in the pipeline's own runtime machinery, not a defect in 017's code, and no violation exists today (grep of runtime/src); the test's doc comment claims only the short forms and is accurate. Deferring it would reopen a done spec for a hardening nobody has asked 017 to carry. (decided by the rule)
- other — hydration of the 21 `--{name}-json` flags is wired by hand in main.rs, and `every_mcp_argument_has_a_cli_form` only proves a flag exists. Only fired-json (process-waivers) and fix-rounds-json/absent (write-review) are driven through a hydrator by a test; no test or script in runtime/tests, runtime/src or scripts/ drives the other write-review fields (findings, observations, applied/expired/new waivers, expired-decisions, decided-by, unread), write-analysis's four, or prune-project's replace through their hydrator, so deleting one hydrate_field line leaves the suite green and a CLI write-review silently drops that argument. — `runtime/src/main.rs:339-415` — **waived**: Pre-window code in the pipeline's own machinery; the shared hydrate_field mechanism is tested and each call is one line read in this review (all 21 fields hydrated: 9 write-review, 4 write-analysis, 2 process-waivers/decisions, 3+2 manifests, 1 prune-project). A per-flag test is new work for spec 022's task 24 surface, not a contract break of 017. (decided by the rule)
- other — criterion-label-backfill.md Notes cite `700 criteria across 49 specs in this repository's own backfill`; by git, commit eaa3908c assigned 663 labels across 49 spec files, and 700 is the count of labelled criteria in the tree at that commit (663 assigned plus 37 already hand-labelled). The figure states no method, so a reader cannot tell which it counts. — `framework/migrations/criterion-label-backfill.md:24` — **waived**: Outside the window's hunk (74f6361c changed only step 1), historical illustration in an adopter-facing note that gates nothing, and no loaded rule maps to it; a recorded count's method is a constitution grounding norm, not a rule. (decided by the rule)
- other: CFG-ENV-001 does not say whether an empty value on an optional value with a default is absent or malformed — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **waived**: predates the widening for environment variables and is unchanged by it; which way an empty optional value falls is a new decision, and CFG-ENV-008 already fails a present malformed value
- other: "at startup" in CFG-ENV-003, CFG-ENV-005 and CFG-ENV-008 does not cover a configuration file a running process reads again — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **waived**: a value first read later is validated when it is read, the reading 063 applies on every call, and no spec here reloads a file mid-process
- other: CFG-ENV-008's error names the setting and the value but not the file, which CFG-ENV-003 and CFG-ENV-005 now name for a file key — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **waived**: naming the file is a new requirement on a rule today's answers did not widen; the setting and the value already locate the defect
- other: CFG-CONST-004 places a value's range bounds alongside its default constant, which a required value has none of — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **waived**: predates the widening for required environment variables; where a required value's bounds live is a new decision, and the bounds must still be named constants
- other: CFG-ENV-011's Source pairs IEC 80000-13 with a NIST page that credits the binary prefixes to IEC 60027-2 — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **waived**: BIPM's VIM attributes the binary prefixes to IEC 80000-13 and NIST to its predecessor IEC 60027-2; together the two citations are accurate
- other: the pre-signed-URL MAY covers object storage only, so another signed-URL scheme, a CDN's or an application's own HMAC-signed link, falls under BE-API-013 and FE-STORAGE-003 — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: the owner's 2026-10-20 answer scoped the MAY to a pre-signed URL for one object and one method; whether other signed-URL schemes fit that scope is a new decision
- other: BE-AUTHN-007's Verification asks for legitimate-recovery paths during lockout, which its Statement does not name — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: no flag condition fires on its absence; adding it to the MUST or removing it changes scope, and no accuracy defect requires either
- other: BE-API-001's Statement requires frame protection on all HTTP responses while its Verification table sets frame-ancestors on HTML responses only — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: predates this window; OWASP's HTTP Headers Cheat Sheet, which BE-API-001 cites, says X-Frame-Options gives no security on a redirect or a JSON API response, while its REST Security Cheat Sheet recommends frame-ancestors 'none' on API responses, so aligning the two decides what the rule requires
- other: CFG-ENV-005, widened to every source, does not say whether a value read from several sources is range-checked as resolved or at each source — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **waived**: the value validated at startup is the one CFG-ENV-007's resolution yields, the reading CFG-ENV-003 states for presence, and no spec here reads a ranged value from two sources
- other: configuration-cross.md's header names only analyze as enforcing it; which review pass applies the file is open — `framework/rules/configuration-cross.md` — **deferred** to `specs/020-code-review/scenarios/every-loaded-rule-file-has-a-pass.md`
- other: FE-SEMHTML-001 and FE-ARIA-001 both forbid a div with role=button — `framework/rules/accessibility-frontend.md` — **waived**: consistent overlap of two MUSTs with no contradiction; consolidating them changes rule ownership, which 069's clarify owns
- other: FE-KBD-002 and FE-CONTRAST-001 both require 3:1 for focus indicators — `framework/rules/accessibility-frontend.md` — **waived**: the owner decided FE-KBD-002's MUST carries the 3:1 ratio (069 Resolved Questions, 2026-10-19); the overlap is consistent
- other: accessibility-frontend.md's first header paragraph is long — `framework/rules/accessibility-frontend.md` — **waived**: style only; the content is accurate, and 069's first open question owns what the header claims about conformance
- other: FE-STORAGE-006 confines its Cache API MUST to service-worker code — `framework/rules/security-frontend.md` — **waived**: the rule is accurate as scoped; widening a MUST to page scripts is a new requirement for the owner, not an accuracy defect
- other: BE-REPR-001's Verification names specs/system.md for the API-wide representation — `framework/rules/api-backend.md` — **waived**: the Statement's one representation across the API needs a declared one to be checkable; the Verification names where it is declared
- other: BE-SCHEMA-001's SDL-versus-introspection split and BE-ERRENV-001's overlap with BE-ERR-002 — `framework/rules/api-backend.md` — **waived**: both splits are recorded as intended in 068; low
- other: BE-AUTHN-010's factor list reads as closed — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: the sentence carries no obligation keyword and the Verification enforces MFA presence, the SMS-sole ban and no skip path, consistent with BE-AUTHN-021; whether push is acceptable is a substantive owner change
- other: security-backend.md's header lists its categories in a different order from its sections — `framework/rules/security-backend.md` — **waived**: the category set matches; no artifact specifies the order, and the header matches 008's data-model table order
- other: BE-COORD-001's Verification asks a plan to say whether a lock guards correctness or efficiency — `framework/rules/concurrency-backend.md` — **waived**: the Statement's MAY turns on that classification; the Verification asks for exactly the fact it needs
- other: BE-QUERY-004, BE-POOL-005 and BE-TXN-004 repeat PostgreSQL background sentences — `framework/rules/performance-backend.md` — **waived**: the repeated sentences are accurate and not contradictory; each rule stands alone
- other: concurrency, observability, reliability, quality and configuration section titles are sentence case — `framework/rules/concurrency-backend.md` — **waived**: 017's and 036's data models record their files' category names in sentence case (017 task 38, an owner decision); 008's title-case bullet describes the security files
- other: framework-checks step (m) tests the pins check on fixtures only, so a pull request adding a tag-pinned uses: merges green until the weekly run reports it — `.github/workflows/framework-checks.yml` — **waived**: The owner chose the weekly currency check as the pins' update process (022 task 3, option c); a per-pull-request pin lint is not part of it and would be its own decision.
- other: runs-on: ubuntu-latest is mutable and now supplies framework-checks' Node too — `.github/workflows/framework-checks.yml` — **waived**: The owner chose the runner image's Node (022 task 3, option a), and a GitHub-hosted image has no immutable reference to pin.
- other: "shipped" still describes the CI template in 017's AC24, plan.md's Affected Files row and 022's task 3 text — `specs/017-derive-dont-ask/spec.md` — **waived**: AC24 calls it a shipped example, the Affected Files row says adopters copy it, and 022's task 3 text is a spent task; none claims it is installed.
- other: 017's plan and 072's Affected Files still name the old import form — `specs/017-derive-dont-ask/plan.md` — **waived**: each records that spec's own edit at the time, a decision record, not a claim about the tree today
- convention: a #fragment containing a dot is cut at the dot on a fold into a scenario — `runtime/src/primitives/rewrite_spec_links.rs` — **waived**: GitHub's heading slugs carry no dots, so such a fragment names no heading a link in this corpus can reach; a contrived input
- convention: a .githooks linked into a host configuration directory inside the project is not judged by host_config::check_destination — `runtime/src/primitives/rewrite_spec_links.rs` — **waived**: a contrived layout; the write stays inside the project, and the referrer list is fixed to five files none of which is a host's configuration
- convention: a kept-tails entry gives the reference from the spec root, without a prefix kept on the line — `runtime/src/primitives/rewrite_spec_links.rs` — **waived**: its path and line locate the reference, and the check reports the same span from the root
